Document 069: 2026年10月FOMCプレビュー ～インフレ認識とAI投資の持続性～

### Page 1

US Trends

2026 年10 月 22 日

1/5 
2026 年 10 月 FOMC プレビュー 
～インフレ認識と AI 投資の持続性～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  前田 和馬 
（℡：050-5473-3888）

（要旨） 
○ 10 月 FOMC（10/27～28 開催）では政策金利が 3.75～4.00％に据え置かれる可能性が高い。
FRB は 9 月に約 3 年振りとなる利上げに踏み切った一方、追加の政策調整を急がず、今後の
経済指標を見極める姿勢を示すとみられる。 
○ ウォーシュ議長の記者会見では、予想を上振れるインフレ動向に関する評価、及び一部のAI
クラウド業者による信用不安が今後の設備投資に与える影響を巡って、何らかの見解が示さ
れるかが注目される。もっとも、市場で織り込まれる12 月 FOMCの利上げを巡っては、具体
的な言及を避ける可能性が高い。 
○ FRBは5つのタスクフォースのうちの生産性と雇用（Productivity and Jobs）部会におい
て、11月16 日にサンフランシスコで公聴会を開催する予定だ。年末までに取りまとめられ
る提言や議論の進捗に関して、ウォーシュ議長から何らかの言及があるかが注目される。

### Page 2

2/5 
・据え置きが見込まれる 10 月FOMC 
2026 年10月FOMC（10/27～28開催）において、FRBは賛成多数で政策金利（3.75～4.00%）を据
え置く可能性が高い。FRBは 9 月FOMCにて米国経済の堅調な推移やインフレ高止まりを踏まえ、23
年 7 月以来となる0.25%の利上げに踏み切った。また、同時に公表されたドットチャートで年内に
追加で 1回の利上げ見通しが示された。8 月CPI及びFRBのタカ派的なスタンスを踏まえて、金融市
場は連続利上げの可能性を徐々に織り込んでいったものの、FRB高官は9月末から10 月初めにか
けてこれに慎重なスタンスを示した。例えば、9/29にウィリアムズ総裁は「急ぐ必要はなく、情
報を集める時間がある」 、10/1 にジェファーソン副議長は「 （政策判断には）もう少し時間がかか
るかもしれない」とそれぞれ述べた。 
この結果、本稿執筆時点におけるFF金利先物市場（Fed Watch）において、83.9%の確率で金利
据え置きが見込まれる一方、0.25%ptの利上げ予想は16.1%に留まる（資料1）。なお、金融市場は
12 月FOMCにおける0.25%の利上げに加えて、2027年末に向けて更に 2回程度の利上げを織り込ん
でいる（資料 2）。この場合の合計利上げ幅は+1.0%ptとなり、9月時点のドットチャート（26年に
計 0.5%ptの利上げを行い、27 年は据え置き）を上回る。もっとも、過去の利上げ局面ではそれが
1～2 回に留まることは稀であり、金融市場はこうした過去の事例も一部に織り込んでいるとみら
れる（詳細は 2026年 9月 18日付け「ＦＲＢの過去の利上げサイクルと長期金利の行方」）。

資料 1：10月 FOMCにおける利上げ織り込み 
  資料 2：市場が予想する各FOMCにおける政策金利 の確率分布（10/21時点）

地区で小幅から緩やかに拡大した（2 地区がmoderate growth、3地区がmodest growth、4 地区が

### Page 3

3/5 
slight growth） 」とまとめられた。また、 「労働市場は総じて変化がなかった」と指摘した。一
方、「物価上昇は3 地区で力強く上昇し、8 地区で緩やかに上昇した。8地区のうち3地区では投
入価格の上昇圧力が強まっている」とインフレ高止まりへの懸念が強調された。また、複数の地
区でインフレによる生活費負担の増大が賃金の上昇圧力になっている可能性が指摘された。 
10 月FOMCまでには10/27に 9 月耐久財受注や10月コンファレンスボード消費者信頼感指数など
が公表されるものの、10月FOMCの政策判断に大きな影響を与える可能性は低い。

・インフレ認識、AI投資の持続性、タスクフォース 
9 月FOMCの声明文では「経済活動は堅調に拡大している」との景気認識が維持された。一方、 「イ
ンフレ率は高止まりしている（7 月時点：インフレ率は2%目標に対して高止まりしている。これは
エネルギーを含む一部セクターにおける供給ショックを通じた価格上昇を反映している） 」と、供
給ショックに関する言及が前回から削除された。10 月FOMCにおいても、こうした景気・物価認識は
概ね維持される可能性が高い。一方、物価認識に関して、足下のインフレ減速の鈍化や加速の兆し
を指摘する、或いはインフレ高止まりの背景として需要の強さに言及する場合、タカ派的なスタン
スと捉えられるだろう。 
ウォーシュ議長は記者会見において、次回 12 月FOMCに関する具体的な言及を避けるとみられる
一方、下記3 点に関して新たな見解が示されるかが焦点となろう。 
まず、足下のインフレ認識に変化があるかだ。9 月FOMCの記者会見で議長は「インフレ率は高止
まりしており、その状態はあまりに長く続いている」や「あまりに多くの品目が 3％を超えるイン
フレ率にある」と述べた。また、同会合の議事要旨では何名か（some）の参加者が「AI普及を通じ
て、需要が中期的に供給を上回り、インフレ上昇への圧力となる可能性がある」と指摘した。その
後公表された 9 月のコアCPIは予想を上振れるなど、高関税による財価格の押し上げやエネルギー
高による輸送サービスへの波及の兆しがみられる。ウォーシュ議長がこうした需要と供給双方のイ
ンフレ要因に関して、新たな見解を示すのかが注目される。 
次に、AI投資の持続性への見方だ。FOMC後の10/29に公表される7～9月期実質GDP成長率は前期
から加速すると見込まれ、AI関連を中心とした設備投資の拡大、及び資産効果（株高）による消費
の堅調さが示されるとみられる。ウォーシュ議長は 10/16 の講演において「世界経済は 5～6 か月
前に予測できなかったほどの回復力を示している」と述べ、エネルギー高や利上げに対する経済的
な耐性を強調した。一方、足下ではAIモデル開発業者などに計算資源を提供する「ネオクラウド企
業」の一部で信用不安が浮上しており、データセンターを中心としたAI関連投資が減速するとの懸
念が強まっている。ウォーシュ議長がAI投資の先行き、及び一部企業の信用不安が金融市場全体に
与える影響に関して、どのような見解を示すのかが焦点となる。 
最後に、タスクフォースの進捗状況が部分的にでも示され、2027年以降の政策運営に関するヒン
トがみられるかだ。ウォーシュ議長は就任後の6月に 5 つのタスクフォース（①コミュニケーショ
ン、②バランスシート政策、③既存データの活用、④変化する時代の生産性と雇用、⑤FRBのインフ
レ枠組み）の設置を発表し、FRB改革を推進する姿勢を示した。5つのタスクフォースは秋までに何

### Page 4

4/5 
らかの成果を得たうえで、2026 年末までにFRBへ提言をまとめる予定だ。ウォーシュ議長は 10/16
の講演にて、11 月 16 日にサンフランシスコで生産性と雇用に関する公聴会を開催すると述べてお
り、これを含むタスクフォースの進捗状況が開示されるか注目される。

・FRB高官発言

10/1 ジェファーソン副議長* 
「足下の地政学的な環境、 及び想定以上に強い総需要を踏まえると、
インフレリスクは上振れ方向に傾いている」と追加利上げの可能性
を指摘した一方、「（政策判断には）もう少し時間がかかるかもし
れない」と述べるなど、追加利上げを急がない姿勢を示した

ミネアポリス連銀 
カシュカリ総裁* 
米国経済と労働市場が堅調であると述べたうえで、「現状の金融政
策が景気抑制的ではない」ことを指摘した。一方、10月FOMCにおけ
る政策判断への明言は避けた 
10/8 ウォラー理事* 
「経済指標が予想通りに推移するならば、 インフレ率を+2%に戻すた
めに追加利上げが実施されると予想する」と述べた一方、「利上げ
は連続して行う必要はない」と強調するなど、10月FOMCでは政策を
据え置くべきとの考えを示した

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
5/5
10/12
クリーブランド連銀
ハマック総裁*
インフレ持続の背景として、企業投資の急拡大や資産効果を通じた
消費押し上げによる需要増を指摘した。こうした需要をある程度減
速させる必要性を強調したほか、投資需要を抑制することで雇用を
悪化させずにインフレを抑制できる可能性を指摘した
10/13
ニューヨーク連銀
ウィリアムズ総裁*
年内に追加で1回の利上げを支持した一方、2027年に関しては追加利
上げの必要はないと明言した。この背景として、来年はエネルギー
価格がインフレを押し上げないこと、及び関税の影響が一服するこ
とのほか、足下の米国経済が全体として過熱していないことを指摘
した
10/14
サンフランシスコ連銀
デイリー総裁

「（利上げが）必要となる可能性が高いが、必要な時間はある」と
述べるなど、10月FOMCの利上げに慎重な姿勢を示した。また、予想
を上振れた9月CPIを巡って、 「インフレリスクは依然として高いが、
リスクは現実に起きたわけではない」と、基調的なインフレ動向に
大きな変化が起きていないとの認識を示唆した
10/16 ウォーシュ議長*
AI関連を中心に「投資の急増」が生じていると指摘し、世界経済が
エネルギーショックに対する想定以上の強靭性を示しているとの楽
観的な見方に言及した。一方、先行きの政策決定には「予断を持た
ない」と述べるなど、10月FOMCに関するヒントは与えなかった
注：*は 2026 年に投票権を持つFOMC メンバー。
出所: FRB、各地区連銀HP、各種報道より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

研 究  報 告                
義隆(2458.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
深受 NB 出貨趨緩影響，短期營運受壓抑 
 投資評價與建議 
NB客⼾拉貨⼒道趨弱將壓抑整體營運動能 ， 無⼈機相關產品為主要成⻑來源 ，
2027年營運展望保守，給予30倍PER*2027年EPS評價。 
 重點評論分析 
4Q26 NB產業承壓，營運同步面臨逆風：NB客⼾拉貨保守，雖有短/急單，但
延續性恐不佳 ， 僅有無⼈機相關產品表現較佳 ， 推估營收16億元 ， QoQ-27.9% ，
且仍持續面臨供應鏈成本壓⼒，推估毛利率43.9%，EPS 0.51元。 
短期NB缺料問題難解，2027年營運成⻑動能恐受限：展望2027年，IDC預估
PC出貨量YoY-4.1% ， 而義隆營收約80%以上與NB相關 ， 因此短期公司營運亦
同處於逆風，主要動能來自非NB產品的無⼈機：公司與中研院合作，利用自身
演算法優勢，推出Mini LED local dimming(區域調光)與ISP產品，1H26出貨
以ISP為主，ASP約5~6美元，7月已開始出貨AI Box，ASP約300~500美元，
已通過雷虎測試並開始出貨，2H26 ASP明顯提升，為2026年最佳成⻑動能，
也是推升AI應用占比提升主要動能，2026年AI營收YoY+40%，也是未來公司
重心，且公司具備MIT優勢，將積極爭取國防訂單，未來具成⻑潛⼒。整體而
言，短期NB客⼾拉貨恐保守，主要仰賴無⼈機等非NB產品推升，營運成⻑較
受限。 
 投資風險 
NB需求進⼀步下滑或非NB貢獻不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況  
3Q26營運受NB影響而趨緩，獲利低於預期：3Q26營收22.2億元，QoQ-30.8%，主因1H26 NB客⼾提前拉貨及
缺料造成拉貨⼒道下滑，毛利率44.7%，主因產品組合轉差及供應鏈成本提升(包含模組的被動元件及PCB漲價)，
費用因嚴控成本而下滑至6.96億元，業外主要來自金融評價損失，EPS 0.58元，優於預期。 
4Q26 NB產業承壓，營運同步面臨逆風：NB客⼾面臨CPU、記憶體缺料問題，拉貨保守，雖有短/急單，但延續
性恐不佳，僅有無⼈機相關產品表現較佳，推估營收16億元，QoQ-27.9%，且仍持續面臨供應鏈成本壓⼒，推估
毛利率43.9%，EPS 0.51元。 
短期NB缺料問題難解 ， 2027年營運成⻑動能恐受限 ：受記憶體與CPU缺貨持續壓抑NB出貨成⻑性 ， 以及NB漲價 、
AI排擠商用NB需求等因素，雖1H26提前拉貨推升公司營運仍佳，然自3Q26起NB客⼾需求趨緩，使得公司營運逐
季下滑，展望2027年，IDC預估PC出貨量YoY-4.1%，而義隆營收約80%以上與NB相關，因此短期公司營運亦同
處於逆風，主要動能來自1)Haptic滲透率持續提升，因Haptic ASP達15美元，明顯優於傳統clickpad 2美元，將
帶動觸控板ASP提升，2026年Haptic營收貢獻約3.5~4億元(2025年約0.6億元)，2027年也將持續提升；2)ADAS
來自電動巴士，2026年出貨double，已有第六位客⼾開始小量出貨，2027年持續成⻑，主要來自政府電動巴士
補助所推升，政府最近也補助電動物流車，2027年確實落地後，公司將有電動物流車的成⻑機會，量也比電動巴
士多很多；3)無⼈機：公司與中研院合作，利用自身演算法優勢，推出Mini LED local dimming(區域調光)與ISP
產品，1H26出貨以ISP為主，ASP約5~6美元，7月已開始出貨AI Box，ASP約300~500美元，已通過雷虎測試並
開始出貨，目前僅作前端光學模組，2026年底將推出避障功能，四鏡頭方案約500美元，十二鏡頭方案將達1,000
美元 ， 2H26 ASP明顯提升 ， 為2026年最佳成⻑動能 ， 也是推升AI應用占比提升主要動能 ， 2026年AI營收YoY+40% ，
也是未來公司重心，且公司具備MIT優勢，將積極爭取國防訂單，未來具成⻑潛⼒。整體而言，短期NB客⼾拉貨
恐保守，主要仰賴無⼈機等非NB產品推升，營運成⻑較受限。 
2027年營運動能受限，維持中立評等：NB客⼾拉貨⼒道趨弱將壓抑整體營運動能，無⼈機相關產品為主要成⻑來
源，2027年營運展望保守，維持中立評等，給予30倍PER*2027年EPS評價。

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We attended Fositek’s investor meeting following its 3Q26 
results announcement on 3 November. We believe the company will benefit 
from the AI server trend with a rising liquid-cooling penetration rate in the 
following years. With rising server revenue over 2026-28E given its share-
gain potential, we reaffirm our Buy (1) rating. See our previous memo 3Q26 
revenue largely in line with expectations, 7 October 2026.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results beat our estimates and Bloomberg 
consensus. Fositek’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD4,502m (+20.4% QoQ, 
+26.2% YoY), a record high, was in line with our estimate and the 
Bloomberg consensus. Its 3Q26 gross margin came in at 38.0% (vs. 34.0% 
in 2Q26 and 27.7% in 3Q25), also a record-high level, above our estimates 
(34.3%) and the consensus (36.5%), mainly on manufacturing process 
improvements and better-than-expected product mix (server revenue 
accounted for 81.6% in 3Q26, up from 54.9% in 2Q26 and 56.4% in 1Q26). 
Its operating margin of 32.4% (vs. 27.9% in 2Q26 and 22.2% in 3Q25), 
another record high, beat our estimate (28.9%) and the consensus 
(31.2%), on better opex control. Thus, its operating profit of TWD1,460m 
(+39.9% QoQ, +84.2% YoY) beat our estimate and the consensus by 
11.0% and 3.3%, respectively. Its net profit of TWD1,242m (+46.3% QoQ, 
+80.8% YoY) beat our estimate and the consensus by 15.9% and 6.5%, 
respectively. Its 3Q26 EPS finished at TWD18.11. Its 3Q26 product mix 
was: server (81.6%, +92.7% YoY), foldable phone (15.4%, -53.1% YoY), 
notebook (4.5%, -28.1% YoY) and others (0.6%, -10.8% YoY).

Brighter 2027 outlook, driven by server segment. Following the ramping 
up of GPU and ASICs projects in 2H26, we expect a stronger 2027 revenue 
outlook vs. 2026, along with gross margin expansion. With the rise in the 
liquid-cooling penetration rate, quick disconnect (QD) shipments per rack 
and QD spec upgrade for ASP expansion, we forecast its revenue to grow 
by 40.3% YoY in 2026 and 59.2% YoY in 2027. We raise our 2026-28E 
EPS by 9-29% on our more aggressive revenue and gross- and operating-
margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and lift our 12M TP to 
TWD2,910 (from TWD2,615), still based on a target PER of 32x (vs. its 
past-3-year range of 9-42x [average of 24x]), translating to a 0.6x PEG 
over 2026-28E on our 1-year forward EPS. Key downside risks: lower-than-
expected market share for key clients; slower-than-expected penetration 
rate of foldable phones; and geopolitical risks.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 8% above the Bloomberg consensus 
likely as we are more aggressive on revenue.

3 November 2026

Brighter 2027 outlook driven by server segment

➢ 3Q26 results beat our estimates and Bloomberg consensus 
➢ Stronger revenue outlook in 2027 with gross margin expansion  
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating and raising 12M TP to TWD2,910

Company profile 
Fositek is a provider of foldable phone hinges, foldable laptop/tablet hinges, AR/VR glasses hinges, 
ultra-thin laptop hinges and keyboard hinges, as well as hinges for medical instruments and automobile 
applications. Its major clients include Huawei and Motorola.

ESG analysis

Update Date: 7 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
The CEO and chairperson of Fositek are not the same individual, and we see no issue with its
board quality. Besides, we note that the chairperson has 24 years of experience in this industry
and took over the role of chairman at Fositek in 2014, which is a positive sign from a governance
perspective.
Capital management 2 Fositek's dividend payout ratio was 40-60% over 2022-25, due likely to its capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions (mainly for AVC and SZFSD) was 0-16% of total revenue during 2022-25, and we found no suspicious activity.
E Supply chain management 1
Fositek maintains smooth communication channels with its suppliers and executes its business
operations effectively. In response to ESG issues, Fositek actively implements supply chain
management, striving to optimise the overall environment through product design, raw-material
procurement, manufacturing, transportation, packaging and recycling processes. It integrates
upstream and downstream suppliers to establish a green supply chain. For example, Fositek has
required its suppliers to sign environmental protection agreements since 2021, committing to
comply with hazardous substance control regulations.
G Data security 2
To protect company information and customer data, Fositek has passed the IEC/ISO 27001
information security management system certification and will continue to enhance execution in
various management areas.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

2027 price hike should be case by case amid push back from some customers:
Management said 2027 pricing negotiations could be mixed across customers even
though capacity is nearly fully loaded. Some need more capacity in 4Q26 and are
willing to pay the new post-hike wafer price. However, the company still faces push
back from some customers, likely tied to their inventories. Our recent checks
suggest  Tier 1 foundry L TA wafer pricing into 2027 could be less than 10%, below

the buyside bogey of a 25-30% blended hike. However, for some tier 2 customers,
the price hike could indeed be around 25-30%. We believe the average like-to-like
L TA wafer price could only increase by  15-20% in 2027. Stay EW.
SOI demand exceeds expectation: The company said 12-inch SOI demand already
exceeds its current expansion plan. The priority is to debottleneck and ramp existing
Missouri capacity. More than half of the newly installed tools have qualified for

volume production, and RF SOI and silicon-photonics products have entered volume
production.
4Q26 could be relatively muted: We expect 4Q26 to be relatively muted since
wafer pricing remains tied to older agreements, existing capacity is fully loaded, and
new capacity depreciation would continue to weigh on margin.
With this report, Lucas Wang assumes coverage of GlobalWafers.

Still Better Supply/Demand Dynamics into 2027-28e
3Q26 earnings and 4Q26  outlook review: 3Q26 results missed both our and Visible
Alpha (VA) consensus estimates given the July fire at the Novara, Italy facility, which
disrupted 8-inch wafer production and related SOI output. We expect 4Q26 to be
relatively muted since wafer pricing remains tied to older agreements, existing capacity is
fully loaded and new capacity depreciation would continue to weigh on margin. We had

expected 4Q26 gross margin to be around 25-26%, but we have now reduced that to 22-
23%.

Micron/GWC deal marks a new milestone for GWC
Micron Technology and GlobalWafers announced a deeper strategic partnership on July 9,

2026, centered on a 10-year supply agreement and US$500mn of strategic financing from
Micron to support GlobalWafers' new 12-inch silicon wafer facility in Sherman, Texas. On
its latest earnings call, GWC board proposed executing that financing as up to US$500
million of 10-year privately placed convertible bonds, with Micron as the sole subscriber,
carrying a 0.5% coupon. The agreement secures significant raw silicon wafer capacity for
Micron's long-term manufacturing plans, with the financing linked to the buildout of

GlobalWafers America's (GWA) Sherman facility.
GlobalWafers Chairwoman Doris Hsu described the agreement as the largest and longest
contract in the company's history. The two companies will also collaborate on next-
generation wafer technologies and process innovation. The Sherman plant is notable as
the only US-based source of advanced 12-inch wafers.
The agreement forms part of Micron's broader US$3bn commitment to strengthen the US
semiconductor supply chain and accompanies its target of manufacturing 40% of its

DRAM output domestically by the mid-2030s. We believe GWC is well positioned to

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
support ongoing foundry and memory capacity expansion in the US, not only for Micron
but also for TSMC and Samsung, which are already GWC's two largest customers. We
currently assume Micron/GWC L TA pricing could be US$70-80 on average for the polished
wafer (10-20% above current L TA pricing after discounts), reflecting the higher US cost
base. This would be broadly in line with, or slightly above, L TAs signed in 2021-22.
Current inventory levels remain higher than during the 2021-22 upcycle

when existing L TAs were signed
Notably, customer inventories of 12-inch wafers remain well above levels seen during the
previous industry upcycle. According to SUMCO's inventory trend data (see Exhibit 3 ),
inventory volumes and turnover days were near the lowest levels of the 2014-26 period
during 2021-22, reflecting a lean and highly utilized supply chain. By contrast, the current
cycle presents the opposite picture: shipment volumes are approaching record highs, but

customer inventories are also near record levels, with inventory days and absolute
inventory exceeding those seen even at the peak of the 2021-22 upcycle.
This backdrop differs meaningfully from the 2021-22 environment and, in our view,
supports a more moderate and delayed pricing recovery in 2027 rather than a repeat of
the broad-based increases seen during the prior cycle. In 2021-22, wafer makers held
significant pricing leverage as customer inventories sat near cycle lows despite record

shipment volumes. Fabs consumed nearly all purchased wafers with minimal inventory
buffers, creating a tight supply environment that enabled wafer manufacturers to
implement meaningful price increases across 12-inch wafers.
While shipment volumes currently remain elevated, customers also hold substantial
inventory. This provides a buffer against demand growth and moderate supply disruptions,
reducing the urgency to secure incremental wafer supply. Historically, this dynamic

weakens suppliers' pricing power. Until inventories normalize meaningfully, wafer makers
are likely to have less pricing leverage than they did entering the 2021-22 upcycle. Our
view is that 2027 L TA negotiations will focus primarily on removing current discounts
rather than driving nominal price increases. We broadly expect 2027 industry L TA pricing
increases of 15-20% in average, with Tier 2 customers' wafer price to be raised 25-30% but
Tier 1 customers would only see 5-15%.

Source: Sumco

Estimate Revisions
We cut our EPS estimates by 14%, 15% and 14% for 2026-28, respectively. The
reductions in our  forecasts primarily reflect lower wafer price hike to Tier 1 customers and
the margin pressure from overseas capacity ramp. However, we also lower our tax rate
assumption from 25% to 20% to reflect the subsidy from the US government.

Valuation Methodology
We lower our price target from NT$900 to NT$770: The price target change mostly
reflects our earnings forecast change over  2026-28. All of of key assumptions in our DCF
model are unchanged, including  a cost of equity of 11% (beta of 1.5, risk premium of 6%
and risk-free rate of 2%), an intermediate growth rate of 13%  and a terminal growth rate
of 4%.
Our bull- and bear-case values are also lowered, from  NT$1,140.00 to NT$975.00 and NT

$450.00 to NT$385.00, respectively, implying P/Es of 32x and 13x 2027e.

View descriptions of Risk Rewards Themes here
NT$975.00
32x 2027e EPS
Semiconductor industry recovers faster than
expected. Pure foundry players' utilization
rates rebound to 100%, and memory players
start to pull in more raw wafers. GWC's
total revenue CAGR picks up to >30% in
2025-28 vs. 8% in 2021-23. Gross margin
recovers to over 40% vs. 37.4% in 2023.
NT$770.00
25x 2027e EPS
Semiconductor industry recovers from a

down-cycle. Pure foundry and memory
players start to see recovery in utilization
rate, while both spot price and LTA amount
also increase gradually. GWC's total revenue
CAGR rises to >15% in 2025-28 vs. 8% in
2021-23. Gross margin stays above 25%.
NT$385.00
13x 2027e EPS
Semiconductor industry recovers more
slowly than expected during this down-
cycle. Pure foundry players start to breach
LTAs and memory players start to cut more
raw wafer orders. GWC's total revenue

CAGR slows to 0% in 2025-28 vs. 8% in
2021-23. Gross margin declines to <20% vs.
37.4% in 2023.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Micron Technology Inc. (MU.O)
30x through-cycle earnings of US $40.00, a premium to history reflecting new opportunities
in AI, in-line with broader semis.
Risks to Upside
n Customers continue to demonstrate an appetite to take on inventory around macroeco-
nomic uncertainty
n Additional wafer intensity of HBM further improves overall supply and demand
n MIcron's HBM share surpasses expectations
Risks to Downside

n Pricing can turn quickly; a falter in end demand with inventories elevated could lead to
a swift price reduction
n HBM demand falters and competition intensifies, pressuring pricing

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
(-) PC OEMs and ODMs: AMD (covered by Joseph Moore) mentioned that PC sell-
through slowed down in late 3Q, particularly in the consumer segment, and that it
estimates PC units will fall in double digits (%) in CY27. This aligns with Morgan

Stanley's estimate at a ~11% y/y decline (report link). AMD also mentioned that it
expects to outgrow the market in CY27.
(+) Server OEMs, ODMs, and component makers: AMD expects CPU TAM to reach
US$220bn by 2030, implying a ~50% CAGR. It also mentioned that its Venice CPU
began shipments in 3Q and cloud providers are deploying it in 4Q.
(+) ABF substrates:  AMD said that it has secured the wafers, substrates, advanced
packaging, and memory needed for much higher 2027 growth, and is planning to add

more. It also mentioned that its supply chain is quite "fungible" across its PC and
server business, but overall it still "has more demand than it has supply."

Key Takeaways
We continue to view negative PC comments as bearish for OEMs and ODMs
positioned in the lower-end consumer segment, like Acer and Compal.
Strong  server CPU unit growth from AMD would be positive for Lotes, Wiwynn,
Unimicron, and NYPCB...
...and indirectly benefit GCE, EMC, TUC, and Co-Tech.
We see positive implications for Lotes if AMD takes share in both the PC and

Server segments.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 3

Alchip (3661.TW)
07 November 2026 Citi Research
3
AI infrastructure demand, supply constraints and 
engineering productivity are key for growth outlook
Despite rising concerns over data center power availability, infrastructure 
deployment delays and potential AI investment moderation, management reported 
no meaningful slowdown in customer demand or changes in development 
schedules. Based on discussions with major customers, Alchip expects strong 
demand extending through 2027–2029, with next-generation AI accelerator 
volumes potentially exceeding current-generation levels substantially. 
Management cautioned that shipment delays could arise from supply-side 
constraints, particularly PCB, substrates, passive components and advanced 
semiconductor capacity, rather than weakening end demand. More importantly,  
large AI accelerator projects generally receive priority allocation, leaving smaller 
customers and traditional applications more exposed to shortages. Meanwhile, AI 
adoption is materially improving Alchip’s engineering productivity, particularly in 
RTL coding, verification, design iteration and analysis of implementation reports. 
Alchip’s CPU and networking projects are progressing on schedule, but 
management believes advanced AI accelerator designs offer substantially greater 
engineering differentiation and revenue opportunities than conventional CPU 
projects.
Looking into 2028, the key limitation could shift from customer demand to Alchip’s 
ability to finance and support larger wafer procurement commitments. 
Consequently, working-capital requirements, customer payment terms and the 
potential need for additional financing will become increasingly important factors 
in determining the company’s achievable revenue scale and profitability. 
We lift our 2027 /2028 earnings projection for Alchip by 14% and 13%, reflecting 
stronger order allocation into next two years. We are maintaining our Buy on the 
stock but lowering our TP to NT$6,000, with lower PER at 27x of 2027 EPS (from 
35x previously), reflecting macro and AI policy uncertainty, in the low end of its 
historical PER.

### Page 7

Alchip (3661.TW)
07 November 2026 Citi Research
7
Alchip
Company description
Alchip is a leading provider of silicon design and manufacturing service,
specializing in ASIC (Application-Specific Integrated Circuit) design,
development, and production. Founded in 2003, the company has >1,000
employees and operates in Taipei, Shanghai, Japan, Korea, and the US. Alchip
serves a wide range of industries, including automotive, industrials, consumer
electronics, and computing.

Investment strategy
We rate Alchip as Buy. We expect Alchip to deliver a strong snapback in
2026/27 with its N3 project ramping up. Moreover, we are also positive over
the long-term growth opportunity from its AI ASIC projects with its major CSP
client. Alchip remains one of the few design service providers within TSMC’s
3DFabric alliance. In our view, if CSPs work more aggressively on their internal
design and become more competitive on product offerings, Alchip would be

well positioned as a back-end/turnkey partner.
Valuation
Our target price for Alchip of NT$6,000 is based on 27x 2027E EPS. 27x target
PER is lower than its historical 3-year PER range, which we see as justified on
account of strong growth potential of its AI ASIC projects with its key CSP
client, reflecting macro and AI policy uncertainty but with better project cycle
and strong growth momentum resuming next year. Our target price equates
to 2026/27E P/B of 6x/5x.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: Giant announced its 3Q26 results after market hours on 9 
November 2026. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating on its improving 
fundamentals and the recovery of the global bike market. We believe 
inventory destocking is coming to an end.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results: missed on operating margin. Giant’s 
3Q26 revenue of TWD16.311bn (-2.1% QoQ, +6.2% YoY) was roughly in 
line with our estimate and the Bloomberg consensus. Its gross margin of 
23.9% beat our estimate and the Bloomberg consensus by 2.9pp and 
1.1pp, respectively, driven by product mix optimisation and new product 
launches in its own brand. However, its operating margin of 3.1% missed 
our estimate and the consensus by 1.4pp and 2.1pp, respectively, due to 
marketing activities for MY27 launches and legal fees related to the WRO. 
Thus, its 3Q26 operating profit of TWD510m (-49.0% QoQ, +28.9% YoY) 
missed our estimate and the consensus by 31.3% and 42.2%, respectively. 
Its 3Q26 net profit of TWD330m (-48.0% QoQ, -5.7% YoY) also missed our 
estimate and the consensus by 39.6% and 47.8%, respectively. Its 3Q26 
EPS finished at TWD0.84.

Demand recovery in Europe and China; a bright 2027. Giant sees 
gradual market recovery in Europe on new model launches and demand 
recovery in China. In the US, Giant continues to work towards having the 
WRO lifted, but will adjust production allocation to maximise the supply to 
meet demand in the US market. Its E-bike revenue accounted for 21% of 
9M26 revenue, which we believe reflects the declining OEM segment. 
Giant aims for its e-bike shipments to decline by 1% YoY, from 516,000 
units in 2025 to 510,000 in 2026, on weaker OE customer demand. For 
2026, it guides for sales to decline by low-single-digit % YoY, with the gross 
margin improving to 21-23% (from 20-21%) and an operating margin of 1-
3%. We lower our 2026-28E EPS by 5-30% after factoring in the 3Q26 
results with more conservative operating-margin estimates on marketing 
expenses.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call with an unchanged 
12M TP of TWD121, still based on our 1-year forward EPS forecast with an 
unchanged target PER of 22x to reflect its improved earnings trajectory, 
and we look for a better year in 2027. Key downside risks: slower-than-
expected e-bike penetration in the EU and macro/tariff uncertainties.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 8-26% below the consensus, likely 
as our revenue and operating margin forecasts are more conservative.

9 November 2026

3Q26 results review: missed on operating margin

➢ 3Q26 net profit missed our estimate and the consensus by 39-48% 
➢ Better-than-expected recovery in Europe and China; a brighter 2027 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating with an unchanged 12M TP of TWD121

Company profile 
Founded in 1972 and listed in 1994 on Taiwan’s main board, Giant is the largest bicycle maker in 
Taiwan, with an annual capacity of c.5m bicycles. In 2025, Asia accounted for 57% of its revenue, 
followed by Europe (33%) and America (7%). Around 74% of its 2025 revenue came from OBM (Giant, 
Liv, momentum) brands and 26% from OEM/ODM (Trek, Scott and Colnago) brands.

### Page 5

5

Giant Manufacturing (9921 TT): 9 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 7 Aug 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and Chairperson are not the same individual. Led by the Liu family, there are 11
members on the board. The founder and former Chairman is now a director, with his niece, Mrs.
Tu, leading Giant. We view Mrs. Tu's leadership to be strong, as she is well-recognised by the
former Chairman and the board for her capabilities, and she is one of the few female
chairpersons to lead a Taiwanese company.
Capital management 2 Giant has maintained its dividend payout ratio between 50% and 100% over the past decade , which we believe to be reasonable considering its global leadership status and expansion needs.
Related party &
transaction 2
Revenue from related-party transactions accounted for 58-62% of total revenue over 2019-25,
with sales mostly from its OBM (ASP varies with products/markets). We observed no suspicious
activity.
S Product quality & safety 1
According to Giant's 2023 ESG report, Giant as the global OEM/OBM leader in the bicycle
industry, product quality and safety are the top priority. We see ongoing R&D expenses and
relevant investment in new models and note that Giant has introduced its "Right Ride" system to
assist customers seeking suitable bike options.
S Supply chain management 1
Giant has developed its Giant production management system that diversifies its capacity
globally and utilises production resources fully to lower political or regional risk. As Giant has
control over its material sourcing, design process and manufacturing/OEM, we believe it has
solid supply chain management capabilities that allow for better cost control than peers.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

營 運 分 析

富世達為 AI基礎設施與高階摺疊機轉軸的關鍵供應商 
富世達股份有限公司成立於民國 2001 年，為奇鋐(3017)持股 16.87%的轉投
資公司，總部與研發中心位於新北新莊，並於越南、中國深圳設有生產基地，
提供各類金屬沖壓設計與製造，主要產品為伺服器組件(滑軌、水冷快接頭)，
與摺疊手機、筆記型電腦、平板電腦之轉軸零件，從消費性電子零組件廠轉
型為 AI基礎設施與高階摺疊機轉軸的關鍵供應商，主要出貨終端包含華為、
Motorola 等摺疊機，與各大AI伺服器平台(NVIDIA、AMD、ASIC等)機櫃用
的滑軌與水冷快接頭。

伺服器組件（QD／滑軌） 摺疊機轉軸 筆電轉軸 其他精密機構件

26Q3 伺服器產品營收占比上升至 81.3%，帶動毛利率大幅優於預期。 
富世達 26Q3 營收 45.0 億元(QoQ+20.4%/YoY+26.2%，優於兆豐前次預期
4%)，因 VR200、ASIC 平台小量拉貨與非奇鋐之快接頭客戶規模上升使伺
服器快接頭營收增長強勁；毛利率 38.0%(季增 3.9ppts/年增 10.3ppts，優於
兆豐預期 2.9ppts)，主因產品組合改善，伺服器產品營收占比超越 80%；營
業淨利 14.6 億元 (QoQ+39.9%/YoY+84.2%)， 稅 後 淨 利12.4 億元
(QoQ+46.3%/YoY+80.8%)，EPS 18.1元(前次預估 15.9 元)。

資料來源：Cmoney、兆豐投顧整理與預估

QD 受惠 GPU與 ASIC雙平台放量及規格升級，為伺服器產品主要成長動能 
26Q3 富世達伺服器產品占營收 81.3%，其中快接頭(QD)約 90%，整體單季
稼動率約 80~90%；而目前新產能開出後 QD 產能已達 1350 萬顆/季。今年
QD 營收貢獻以 GPU 平台為主，明年 ASIC 比重將持續提高，預期 2028 年
ASIC 比重有望超越 GPU；其中(1)AWS Trn3 已配合客戶時程出貨，將於
27H1顯著貢獻營收。(2)Google TPU V9世代有望由現行的 NSP1接頭，變
為導入混用 QD+NSP1接頭，以優化未來光模塊、DIMM 等部件潛在導入水
冷系統後維護的容易性。(3)Meta MTIA 與微軟 MAIA 等 AISC 專案也將於
27H2 出貨帶動 QD 市場規模提升。而在 Rubin Ultra 世代，將導入 ZQD 於
運算托盤(取代部份的 UQD)與 rack manifold之快接頭，且ZQD單顆價值預
估將為現行 MQD與 UQD 單價的兩倍，ZQD 預計 12月進入量產準備。 
規格面，VR200 單櫃 QD 數量約 538 顆(含 rack manifold 88 顆)，Rubin 
Ultra(VR300)將為 574~664顆，預估 QD量價齊揚趨勢延續，為2027~2028
年主要成長動能。

AI 滑軌陸續通過驗證，27年可望隨 VR200與 ASIC 專案量產放量 
滑軌事業目前月產能維持 35 萬套，目前以通用型為主，稼動率約 3 成；隨
明年 AI專案的量產，稼動率可望提升至50~60%以上，毛利率也將隨著 ASIC
水冷專案的放量同步改善，水冷櫃滑軌對承重、緩衝與平行度要求較高，ASP
亦較高，公司預期明年滑軌維持高度成長，後續將視訂單規劃擴廠。

- 4 - 
個股報告

華為摺疊機新品拉貨遞延與記憶體推升價格，轉軸營收短期承壓 
華為新機種受記憶體影響拉貨延後，加上終端需求轉弱，26Q3富世達手機
軸承營收占比降至 15.5%，預期26Q4手機轉軸拉貨動能有限。而華為 26H1
中國摺疊機市占逾 7成，摺疊機累計出貨突破 2,000 萬支；惟記憶體漲價使
單機平均成本增加約 200 美元，9/1 起主力機型最高調漲 17%，後續產品將
朝輕薄化發展並導入新材料，每代價格將重新議價，下一代機種仍由富世達
主供。雖蘋果 iPhone Duo 上市帶動摺疊機話題，將使摺疊機長期接受度與
滲透率同步提高，但短期消費性電子仍受記憶體價格推升單價而影響終端購
買意願。

26Q4 營收下修、毛利率上修，獲利預估小幅調降 
預估富世達 26Q4 營收 54.0 億元(QoQ+20.0%/YoY+35.8%，較原估下修
8.2%，主因摺疊機軸承拉貨不如預期 )；毛利率 38.1%(季增 0.1ppts/年增
9.0ppts，較原估上修 1.1ppts，主因 QD 生產進入規模化，帶動高毛利伺服
器比重上升)；稅後淨利 14.8 億元(QoQ+19.4%/YoY+97.3%，較原估下修
4.7%)，EPS 21.63元(原估 22.7 元)。

### Page 1

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
78 
삼성전자  (005930) 
2026.11.04

STRESS TEST 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

목표주가 37만원, ‘28년 기준으로는 56만원까지 열림 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 37만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27 년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3 배를 적용했다. 이는 ‘00년 이후 25년간 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 
해당한다[표1]. ‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 56%, 5 9%, 4 8%로 지난 25년 평균인 
22%를 크게 상회할 전망이며, 고원형 ROE가 장기간 유지될 것으로 판단한다.  
이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해질 경우 시장의 시야는 ‘28년으로 향할 것이다. 
이 경우 ‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 56만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 43% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -41%를 넘어섰다. 특히 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
이러한 우려가 지속되는 한, 이익의 눈높이가 높아질수록 멀티플은 저렴해질 
뿐이며, DRAM 가격이 예상보다 더 올라갈 것이라는 소식이 주가의 촉매로 
작용하지 못한다. 결국 주가 반등을 위해서는 시장이 상정하고 있는 다운사이클이 
우려만큼 깊지 않을 것이라는 믿음이 필요한 것으로 본다.

STRESS TEST와 HBM 재평가 
① 당사는 고객들이 어느 정도 가격대에서 LTA를 해지하려는 유인이 생길지 
추정해봤다. Micron의 코멘트를 차용하여 ‘22~’23년 수준의 스트레스 테스트를 
시나리오 분석한 결과, 4Q27 고점 대비 DRAM 가격이 60% 수준 빠져야 해지가 
검토될 수 있다[그림2]. 그마저도 다음 공급 부족기의 물량 배정 우선순위 상실 
등 정성적 요인까지 감안하면 실제 해지 가능성은 더욱 낮은 것으로 판단한다. 
② HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 
중장기적으로 확대될 cHBM은 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 
재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 물량과 가격이 결정된다. 수요가 둔화되더라도 범용 
메모리처럼 재고 누적과 가격 급락으로 이어지기보다는 발주 조정이나 계약 
재협상을 통해 영향을 받는 구조다.  
동사 HBM 매출은 ‘26년 28조원에서 ‘28년 215조원으로 증가할 전망이다. 
‘27년 HBM 가격 방향성의 윤곽이 잡힌 현 시점에서, 동사 HBM 매출의 변수는 
가격이 아니라 물량이며, 물량은 고객과 사전에 협의되는 구조다. 과거 메모리 
업황에서는 2년 뒤의 이익을 가늠하기 어려웠으나, 이제는 HBM을 통해 중장기 
실적을 논의할 수 있게 됐다는 점, 그 자체에 주목한다. 
만약 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E (21배, 15배, 12배)를 
적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 19%, 69%, 108%를 설명한다[표3]. 
특히 cHBM이 본격화할 ‘28년에는 HBM만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명될 수 
있다는 의미다. 시장이 통상 받아들이는 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 다르지 
않다.

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간일 전망이다.  
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 업황 지속성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 이 불확실성이 
멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 
구간이 이어질 전망이다. 기회비용은 결국 환원수익률로 보상받아야 하므로, ‘27년 1월 신규 
주주환원 정책이 중요한 변곡점이 될 것으로 판단한다.  
당사는 동사가 ‘27~’29년 FCF의 50%를 주주에게 환원하고, 이 중 70%를 배당, 30%를 
자사주 매입/소각으로 쓰는 것으로 가정하여, 현재 주가 기준 ‘27년 15% 수준의 
주주환원수익률을 기대한다. 미국 10년물 국채 금리가 5% 안팎인 환경에서 무위험 금리의 
3배에 달하는 환원수익률, 그중 배당만으로 10% 수준의 수익률은 그 자체로 희소한 투자 
포인트로 볼 수 있다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는, 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 이후 신규 팹 물량이 
소량 출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 LTA가 이익을 지킨다는 사실이 
숫자로 확인되고 cHBM의 가치가 재평가된다면 순환 할인이 축소될 것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

한화에어로스페이스 (012450)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 방위산업 | 2026. 11. 2  
방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감

방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감 
지상방산 매출 1조 2,450억원으로 전년비 41%, 영업이익 217억원으로 전년비 
96.2% 급감하며 영업이익률은 1.7% 기록. 실적 급감에 대해 사측은 올해 주요 사업
의 납품 일정이 4분기에 집중됨에 따라 3분기 인도 물량이 적었던 것과 대전 사업장 
생산 중단에 따른 영향 때문이라고 설명. 단, 4분기에는 당초 계획에 따라 주요 사업의 
납품 물량이 집중되어 있어 실적이 개선될 것이고 대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 내에 정
상 가돌 하는 것이 목표라고 밝힘 
수주잔고 증가세 지속으로 성장 지속 가능할 것 
3분기 말 수주잔고 35조원으로 전년비 12.9% 증가. 이를 통한 지상방산 부문 성장 
지속 가능할 것으로 판단. 참고로 사측은 내년 매출 15% 수준 성장 전망 유지. 하지
만 금리 상승에 따른 글로벌 업종 평균 밸류에이션 하락에 따른 방산 부문 적용 멀티
플 하향 조정 그리고 상장 연결회사(한화오션, 한화시스템, 쎄트렉아이)와 상장 비연결
회사(한국항공우주)의 주가 하락 반영 영향으로 목표주가는 하향 조정 
8월 미 육군 차륜형 자주포 사업에서 시제 사업자로 선정. 향후 K9MH는 미 육군의 
작전 운용 개념에 맞춰 맞춤 조정을 거친 후에 특별한 결함 혹은 결격 사유가 없으면 
최종 계약 체결 예정. 동시에 미 육군의 궤도형 자주포 사업에도 참여할 계획. 뿐만 아
니라 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등으로 신시장 개척 역시 지속되고 있다는 점도 긍
정적 
 Analyst 이재광 
rheejaekwang @lssec.co.kr

Document 391: Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

Jan Hatzius 
+1(212)902-0394 | jan.hatzius@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC
Global Views: Downside Growth Risks
26 October 2026 | 6:45AM EDT
1. So far in 2026, global growth has been remarkably unremarkable.  Both the world 
as a whole and the G3 economies are growing in line with our estimates of potential 
GDP .  Our interpretation is that fiscal easing and the positive impulse from the AI 
boom have offset the negative impulse from the war in Iran and the lingering drag 
from higher US tariffs.

2. Our standing global GDP forecasts project more of the same in 2027, as lower 
energy prices offset fading tailwinds from fiscal policy and AI.  But the balance of 
risks is on the downside.  The most immediate concern is that, far from subsiding, 
energy prices have again climbed sharply on fears of renewed Middle East escalation.  
The prices of gasoline, diesel, jet fuel, and European/Asian natural gas are now near 
or even above their March/April highs, with likely negative effects on consumers and 
energy-importing economies more broadly.

### Page 2

3. Although we don’t expect significant fiscal policy changes in 2027, the consequences 
of large-scale deficits—coupled with AI-linked borrowing and central bank hikes—have 
started to show up more clearly in higher longer-term interest rates.  The moves have 
been mostly orderly, but the increase since early 2026 averages a sizable 100bp across 
DM yield curves.  If sustained, we estimate that this increase will subtract a cumulative 
0.6% from GDP via weakness in housing, consumer durables, and business investment.  
(Only part of the drag is incorporated in our standing forecasts because we expect bond 
yields to pull back as central banks deliver fewer hikes than priced in markets.)

Exhibit 2: Oil Price Risks Are Skewed to the Upside

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research
26 October 2026   2
Goldman Sachs Global Views

### Page 3

4. The growth impulse from the AI boom was set to peak in late 2026 or early 2027 even 
before concerns around the sustainability of the AI investment boom intensified in 
recent weeks.  Although AI investment is still rising rapidly, we expect the increase as a 
share of GDP to slow over the next year.  Alongside the fading boost from last year’s 
increase in US depreciation allowances, this will weigh on capital spending growth, 
especially in the US.  AI has also boosted US consumer spending growth via a positive 
wealth effect worth an estimated 0.4pp (or 0.25% of GDP) over the past year.  This 
impact is likely to decline over the next year, gradually if stocks perform in line with our 
strategists’ reasonably upbeat expectations but more sharply if AI-related stocks 
continue to decline.

### Page 4

5. Our own base case remains that AI investment will ultimately pay off in terms of higher 
productivity and economywide profits.  However, one key channel for this payoff is labor 
market disruption, which has negative near-term macroeconomic effects.  And while DM 
labor markets still look solid in aggregate, we are starting to see clearer signs that the AI 
impact is spreading beyond the technology sector.  Across nonmanufacturing industries, 
the share of US firms that have adopted AI in regular business functions now shows a 
statistically significant negative correlation with employment growth and a statistically 
significant positive correlation with unemployment.

6. The Fed’s recent signals—explicit from Williams/Jefferson and implicit from Warsh—
effectively rule out a hike on October 28.  But the FOMC still looks very likely to deliver 
another 25bp hike to 4-4.25% on December 9 because spot inflation remains too high.  
And while we continue to expect significant disinflation next year on the back of 
reduced impulses from tariffs, energy prices, and AI-related prices, the risks to our 
forecast are on the upside, most obviously in energy.  Higher inflation could result in

another hike in March, but either way we see renewed cuts in late 2027/early 2028.

7. Like the Fed, the ECB will likely hold rates steady next week and deliver a final hike (to 
2.75%) at the December meeting.  However, the turbulence in the French bond market 
poses somewhat greater downside risks to that call than in the US.  The turbulence has 
tightened financial conditions to a degree that is likely to shave ¼-½pp from annualized 
growth in coming quarters.  And if it intensifies into December, markets might perceive a 
hike as signaling insufficient focus by the ECB on the risk of Euro area fragmentation.  
While the ECB could address this issue by coupling a hike with renewed balance sheet 
expansion, the hurdle for such a move is high, in part because purchases of French 
bonds would inevitably raise concerns about moral hazard with respect to the needed 
fiscal adjustment.

8. Across the English Channel, we expect the MPC to raise Bank Rate by 25bp to 4% on

### Page 6

November 5.  Consistent with our view that the UK economy is stronger than widely 
perceived, GDP growth has surprised on the upside this year and the unemployment rate 
declined to 4.7% in August on a 3-month average basis (with the volatile 1-month rate 
plunging to 4.1%).  Rising inflation and the looming household energy price reset in 
January imply a significant risk of further moves, although market pricing of 4+ hikes 
(including November) still looks excessive.  We also expect hikes in Canada and New 
Zealand this week and in Australia next week.

9. Slightly faster than expected growth in Q3 has pushed up our 2026 GDP forecast for 
China to 4.6%.  But most activity indicators are much weaker.  Both property activity and 
fixed asset investment are contracting year-on-year, and while nominal retail sales 
growth picked up to +1.1% in September, large retailers (whose sales are relatively 
better measured) are still posting substantial year-on-year declines.  With domestic 
demand rising slowly at best, sustained GDP growth in the official 4.5-5% target range 
will require ongoing steep increases in the trade surplus.  But the surplus is already at 1% 
of global GDP , and further increases will eat more noticeably into output and 
employment abroad.  Ultimately, China’s trading partners may respond by imposing 
trade barriers that weigh on Chinese exports and GDP , although the measures taken so 
far have been limited.

Goldman Sachs Global Views

### Page 7

10. Our strongest global view is that central banks will deliver fewer hikes than currently 
priced.  In addition, our rates strategists flag underweight positioning, diminished 
technical risks, and risk asset pressures as reasons why the recent rally might extend.  
The market’s narrow breadth makes our equity strategists wary of momentum factor 
exposure, but they still expect the S&P 500 to resume its climb after the midterm 
election, as earnings continue to grow solidly.  Our credit strategists remain cautious on 
IG technology bonds but see scope for some near-term reversal in HY given the scale of 
the recent selloff.  Our FX strategists expect the Japanese yen to act as a hedge against 
further AI jitters, in part because such jitters would shrink US-Japan interest rate 
differentials.  And our commodity strategists recommend targeted exposures to oil 
products and European natural gas to hedge near-term escalation risks alongside 
longer-term exposures to copper, gold, and select power markets to benefit from 
structural supply/demand imbalances. 
Jan Hatzius

► 美國9月CPI年增率上升至 3.7%，核心CPI年
增率由 2.4%升至2.6%，核心月增率 0.3%，
高於預期值0.2%。能源是通膨反彈的主因 ，
貢獻整體 CPI月增幅超過一半；能源衝擊雖
為主導 ，但消費韌性使企業保有成本轉嫁能
力，住房與部分服務價格維持黏性 ，需求面
通膨略有提高 ，加上企業資本支出提升經濟
與企業獲利韌性，使總需求不易快速降溫。
► 凱投預期Fed 12月將升息1碼，明年3月再升
1碼，政策利率可能在 4%以上維持更久 。受
到油價高盤 、通膨僵固 ，與財政惡化 、政府
發債籌資逢企業債搶錢等影響 ，美歐日主要
國家公 債利 率近 期 來到高 位 。其中 ， 法國
2027年財政預算欲刪減支出(包括養老金、公
部門工資等)引發民眾反彈 ，衝擊主權信用評
等，法國CDS與法德10年期公債利差擴大 、
歐元走弱 ，美元轉強 ，商品、黃金承壓 。法
國政治 不確定性讓 歐股修正 、法股最疲 ，成
熟國家公債殖利率升高 ，增加企業借貸成本

與股市評價壓力，續為金融市場擾動因子。

資料來源：Bloomberg，Factset，Goldman Sachs，凱基證券整理，註：右上圖之大型雲端服務供應商(CSP)企業為Amazon、Alphabet、Microsoft、Oracle
► 美國第三季財報季展開 ，市場將觀察企業在
高利率環境下的獲利表現 。根據FactSet數據
顯示 ，標普500指數第三季獲利 成長預估達
29.5%，顯示企業基本面仍具支撐 ，其中科
技 產業 受 惠AI 需求帶動 ， 獲 利 成 長預 估達
65%，持續扮演企業獲利的重要成長動能。
► 聚焦科技類股 ，AI資本支出仍為本輪企業獲
利成長的主要驅動力 。高盛指出 ，第三季AI

基礎建設相關企業將貢獻標普 500獲利增長
的54%。此外，大型CSP之雲端業務營收有
望加速成長 ，反映AI運算及雲端服務需求持
續提升，有助支撐後續資本支出擴張動能。
► 近期市場波動仍受高利率環境影響 ，壓抑投
資人風險偏好與估值擴張空間 ，顯示市場目
前壓力更多來自評價面而非基本面 。隨財報
數據陸續公布 ，若企業持續繳出優於預期的
獲利表現 ，並維持正向財測 ，市場有望逐步
回歸基本面評價 ，使企業獲利成長逐步支撐
股市表現。
美企財報獲利穩健，對股市中長期有撐

評價面疑慮下，股價難以進一步擴張

投資焦點
► 醫療保健 、醫療服務 與生物技術等行業 ，在
景氣不好時 ，市場預期民眾對醫療保健產業
的需求 不易 下降 ， 成為傳 統防禦 型類 股代
表。目前金融環境利率偏高 ，11月美國總統
大選國會恐變天 ，股市波動風險上升 ，觀察
過去當 VIX恐慌指數上揚時 ，醫療保健產業
相較於科技股更穩健，具有更好抗震能力。
► 醫療與生技類股近一年漲幅明顯 ，主因1)生
技股過去一段時間受到高利率 、融資成本及
臨床試驗風險壓抑 ，部分公司估值偏低 。當
臨床數據改善 、或導入AI篩選資料與 藥物，
投資人重新評價未來現金流與價值 。2)大型
藥廠面臨既有藥品專利到期壓力 ，透過收購
取得新藥與研發管線 ，提高有潛力的生技公
司併購價值 ，帶動整體產業估值回升 。3)肥
胖、癌症與慢性病用藥創造長期市場 需求，
創新藥物 (如瘦瘦針 )讓部分醫療公司成長不
再只依賴人口老化，高盛今年7月將全球減肥
藥市場 2030年銷售額預估上調 15%至1,140
億美元，看好此類藥品的長期商業潛力。

醫療生技近年表現佳，估今年3Q醫院客流量年增4%VIX恐慌指數上升時，健護醫療表現相較穩健
市場波動上升、醫療保健表現佳，獲利穩定成長

內部參考文件請勿外流
► 美國醫療費用深受法令影響 ，由於商業雇主
贊助保險 (ESI)和平價醫療法案 (ACA)的自付
額增加 ，以及最高自付費用要求 ，較去年同
期提高 ，使今年第三季美國整體住院人數 、
手術量和醫院客流量均呈現改善 。高盛預估
今年第三季醫院客流量年增4%，加上季節性
因素，下半年醫療保健利用率將較上半年成
長，整體第四季預估獲利穩健 ，甚至可延至
明年，反應在近期XLV ETF股價創新高。
► 展望2027年，各醫療保險計畫普遍優先考慮
透過提高會員保費 ，和要求費用分攤(尤其是
提高會員自付費用上限 )來擴大利潤空間 ，並
持續優化覆蓋範圍和產品組合 ，預估商業雇
主提供的醫療保險 (ESI)保費趨勢，將在明年
加速成長至過去 20 年來的最高水準 。 以
Refinitiv預估明年美國各產業獲利成長來看，
醫療保健和生技相較於今年獲利明顯增加 ，
反而今年獲利極佳的能源和通訊受高基期影
響，獲利增速下滑 。投資人在整體金融環境
波動放大時期，宜以醫療生技股穩中求利。

投資焦點
近3個月漲幅和預估獲利呈正成長，醫療生技股佳
醫療保健和生技預估獲利明年明顯成長，有利股價續揚
資料來源：Bloomberg，凱基投顧，凱基證券整理
預估今明年產業獲利差異，醫療保健和生技成長最大

股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長 ，市場靜待財報結果與展望 ，和對AI資本支出回收影響 。
考量地緣衝突 、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉 ，短期金融市場紛擾不止 ，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度 。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變 ，逢股市拉回時可分批布局 ，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間 ，殖利率偏高環境使 短中期債券提供吸
引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利
差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金

融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高 ，使金價短期承壓 ，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化 ，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。

### Page 17

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

Document 418: Nothing To Fear But Fear Itself And FOMO, FEMO, FOGO & FOJO

Nothing To Fear But Fear Itself And FOMO, FEMO, FOGO & FOJO

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Nothing To Fear But Fear Itself
And FOMO, FEMO, FOGO &
FOJO
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 3 Nov 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Игорь Кеся / Unsplash
I. Three Shades Of Fear
Monday's issue of The Wall Street Journal featured a story titled, "Hedge
Fund Elliott Warns of FOGO: Fear of Getting Obliterated in the Market."
That has inspired us today to coin "FOJO," or fear of jumping out of the
market. FOMO, of course, is the fear of missing out on a bull market. Only
a few months ago, we felt the need to coin "FEMO," or fabulous earnings
momentum to describe this year's earnings-led bull market.

FOMO is reflected in the forward price-to-earnings (P/E) multiple, while
FEMO is captured by forward earnings per share (E). So far this year,
FEMO has been booming, but FOMO has been fading. S&P 500 forward
earnings surged 31.3% ytd through November 2, lifting the index 10.2%
despite a 13.8% contraction in its forward P/E (chart). Earnings
momentum has more than offset multiple contraction.
Ironically, one reason FOMO has declined may be FEMO itself. Investors

fear that analysts' earnings expectations might be irrationally exuberant.
We share that concern. Analysts' consensus S&P 500 operating earnings
are currently projected to reach $494 by 2028, well exceeding our $450
forecast and approaching our $500 target for 2030 (chart).

### Page 3

Sentiment about the AI trade has shifted from euphoria to skepticism as
financial, political, social, and physical constraints on the AI buildout
intensify. Investors fear these could slow AI investment and force analysts
to lower their exuberant earnings expectations.
The market seems to recognize that risk. Despite analysts' soaring long-
term earnings growth (LTEG) expectations this year, the S&P 500's
forward P/E has declined to 18.1 (chart). Investors are increasingly

reluctant to pay up for earnings growth that may prove unsustainable.
Doubts about future FEMO are weighing on today's FOMO. (During the
1999 Tech Bubble, the forward P/E rose faster than LTEG.)
The good news is that FOJO might now be driving the bull market. While
investors are increasingly concerned about the AI trade, they are reluctant

### Page 4

to bail out of the stock market. Instead, they are rotating aggressively out
of technology and into other sectors. We remain bullish, targeting 7,900
for the S&P 500 by year-end, based on our 2027 EPS forecast of $425
and a forward P/E of 18.6.
II. The Roaring Economy
Today's economic data clearly illustrate why we remain fundamentally
bullish. Despite stock market jitters, the US economy continues to roar.
Consider the following:
(1) JOLTS. Job openings rose to 7.5 million in September, the highest

since May (chart). Total hires rose to 5.4 million, the highest since March.
Quits surged to 3.4 million, the highest since March 2025. Layoffs fell to
2.0 million, the lowest since March 2025. The message is clear: More job
openings, stronger hiring, rising quits, and fewer layoffs all point to
strengthening labor market conditions.
Job openings in cyclically sensitive industries are upbeat. Manufacturing
openings rose to 584,000 in September, the highest since December

2023, while construction openings have also been trending higher (chart).
Both industries are benefiting from the AI infrastructure investment boom.
Meanwhile, retail trade openings are trending higher, reflecting strong
consumer demand.

### Page 5

(2) Consumer spending. With the labor market remaining healthy, it is
hardly surprising that consumers continue to spend. Redbook same-store
retail sales rose 9.8% y/y during the week of October 30 (chart).
(3) Durable goods orders. Durable goods orders rose 1.9% m/m in
September. Orders excluding transportation increased 0.5%, while core
capital goods orders (nondefense ex-aircraft), a key gauge of business
investment, rose 0.6% and 13.7% y/y (charts).

### Page 6

The major components of durable goods orders are at record highs
(chart).

### Page 7

Orders for machinery necessary to operate data centers are especially
strong (chart).
Meanwhile, unfilled machinery orders climbed to $155.1 billion in
September, approaching their all-time high and signaling a robust pipeline
of future production (chart).
Unfilled orders for computers and electronic products rose to a record
$162.4 billion in September (chart). The increase highlights robust
demand for AI-related infrastructure.

### Page 8

clause.

Editorial 
Opportunities from the bond sell-off  
Strategy summary: The global bond sell-off is creating tactical 
and medium-term opportunities. In the US, September’s yield 
surge has improved the risk -reward in 10 -year government 
bonds, presenting an attractive entry point for investors with a 
6-12-month horizon. We believe bond and money markets are 
overly hawkish about the Fed outlook, given disinflation is likely 
to return as impact of oil prices and tariffs ease next year. 
In equities, we stick to quality, especially sectors riding the AI -
led earnings boom. The upcoming Q3 earnings season is likely 
to show AI -investments are powering sectors beyond the 
technology and communications services, such as financials, 
materials and power and electrification. We remain constructive 
on the semiconductor and memory segments. We also expect 
the US Dollar to peak in the next few weeks along with short -
to-medium-term US bond yields. This creates opportunities for 
AUD corporate bonds, Emerging Market USD bonds and gold. 
Attractive risk -reward in US 10 -year government bonds.  
The US 10-year bond yield rose more than 50bps in September 
(biggest monthly rise since 2022) and 115bps this year, amid 
expectations of economic resilience, higher Fed rates, fuel -
driven inflation, AI-related capital demand and fiscal concerns. 
Valuations appear close to fair value, improving the 6-12-month 
risk-reward. Bond arithmetic suggests losses of less than 1% if 
the yield rises to 6%, vs. more than 10% gains if it falls to 4.5%. 
Resilient US economic activity, but softer -than-expected 
inflation. September data showed robust US employment 
(jobless claims, ADP) and core capital-goods orders. However, 
consumer confidence remains depressed, real disposable 
income is flat and households are drawing on savings to sustain 
spending. Against this divided backdrop, core PCE inflation - 
the Fed’s preferred gauge - was softer than expected at 0.2% 
m/m and 3.0% y/y. A revised method to measure PCE inflation 
also indicate that annual core PCE inflation was 35bps lower 
than previously reported, narrowing the gap with CPI inflation. 
Markets appear overly hawkish on the Fed. Money markets 
price about 85bps of tightening over the next 12 months. A 
strong labour market (September’s non -farm payrolls data is 
due tonight) may justify one or two further hikes, but aggressive 
tightening after the sharp rise in bond yields – which has 
already tightened financial conditions - could threaten financial 
stability. US small- and mid-cap equities have already fallen 8–
9% from recent peaks over the past couple of months, 
materially underperforming large caps, as energy and 
borrowing-cost concerns intensify. W hile some Fed speakers 
remain hawkish, we  agree with New York Fed President 
Williams’ that there is no urgency to tighten. We see little 
chance of an October Fed rate hike and expect inflation data 
for Sep-Nov determining whether the Fed moves in December. 
Stay with quality equities and AI beneficiaries.  US large 
caps have absorbed the rise in bond yields relatively well, 
supported by expectations of robust earnings growth led by AI-
related investment. We continue to favour quality companies 
and sectors benefiting from this AI cycle. Semiconductors and 
memory remain preferred exposures: structural AI demand 
should extend the memory upcycle, while long -term supply 
agreements provide better revenue visibility. The opportunity is 
also broadening beyond technology and communication 
services into financials, materials, power and electrification. 
Opportunities in AUD corporate bonds and gold.  The USD 
rebound alongside higher US yields offers a tactical opportunity 
to add higher -yielding Emerging Market USD and non -USD 
bonds. We remain positive on AUD corporate bonds, which 
offer a yield premium over comparable US credit. Our 
constructive medium-term AUD/USD view could add returns for 
USD-based investors over the next 6 –12 months. We would 
also use gold’s recent pullback to add exposure, supported by 
sustained Emerging Market central bank demand. We continue 
to view USD 4,000/oz as important technical support for gold.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions 
  Do you expect more RBA rate hikes after its less hawkish 
guidance? What is your view on the AUD and AUD bonds? 
Our view: We expect the RBA to leave rates unchanged, but 
retain the option to hike again should inflation intensify. We 
maintain our medium-term constructive AUD view and maintain 
our opportunistically bullish AUD corporate bonds idea. 
Rationale: The RBA’s forward guidance remains data dependent, 
explicitly allowing further tightening if needed as inflation remains too 
high.  At 4.60%, Australia’s cash rate is currently 60–85bp above the 
Fed’s 3.75–4.00% range. Thus, even with our two additional 25bp s 
Fed hikes expectation, Australian rates would hold a modest positive 
rate differential. We expect the AUD to strengthen once the Fed 
signals that US rates are near their peak, which should allow resilient 
Australian fundamentals to reassert themselves. Key risks are 
persistently high US yields, weaker China growth or a sharper 
deterioration in global risk sentiment. 
We believe risk-reward has become more attractive for AUD bonds 
following the bear -flattening of the AUD yield curve. Markets are 
already pricing in one more RBA hike through end-2027, presenting 
an attractive opportunity to earn a yield even as increasingly 
restrictive financial conditions reinforc e disinflation over time. 
Meanwhile, the positive interest rate differential with the US supports 
the currency outlook, supporting the case for AUD bonds. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  What does the incoming US data tell us about the path for 
US 10-year bond yields from here? 
Our view: The risk -reward in US 10 -year yields has turned 
attractive following their move higher amid incoming US data. 
Rationale: Recent US data has indeed started to show softer-than-
expected inflation. However, market technicals are likely temporarily 
delaying a move lower in bond yields, with reports suggesting the  
rise in yields is creating forced selling amongst hedge funds and real 
estate investment trusts. Nevertheless, we do not expect this to be 
an extended selloff. We have initiated an opportunistic bullish idea 
on US 10-year government bonds this week and continue to believe 
this idea offers an attractive risk/reward on a 6-12-month horizon as 
yields become attractive enough to entice buyer flow to return. On a 
broader allocation basis, we retain our structural preference for 3-7-
year maturities in bonds. 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in the GBP ahead of the UK 
budget announcement next month? 
Our view: We maintain a medium-term constructive GBP view, 
but keep near-term exposure measured ahead of the 28 October 
Budget. We would view any pre -Budget weakness as an 
opportunity to gradually build GBP exposure. 
Rationale: The government’s decision to adjust the pension triple 
lock from 2030 is an early indication of greater willingness to tackle 
longer-term spending pressures, reinforcing Chancellor Healey’s 
commitment to fiscal discipline and meeting the fiscal rules. Markets 
are likely to scrutinise the Budget closely for evidence that this 
discipline extends to near -term tax and spending decisions. A 
credible Budget should help contain pressure on UK government 
bond yields and support GBP. Any fiscal slippage remains a key risk.  
Near term, we prefer measured GBP exposure as elevated US yields 
and repricing towards further Fed tightening could continue to 
support the USD. Over the medium term, we expect GBP to rebound 
as the Fed eventually signals the peak of its tightening cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  How would different US mid-term election outcome 
scenarios affect the outlook for US equities? 
Our view: We believe election outcomes will have a limited 
impact over the next 12 months. We remain positive on US 
equities driven by fundamental earnings growth.  
Rationale: Over the past 50 years, the ruling US President’s party 
has retained control of both chambers of Congress on only two 
occasions (1978 and 2002). All other elections result ed in an 
opposition sweep or a divided government.  
Based on the latest prediction markets, it appears that an opposition 
sweep (both the House and Senate flips from Republican to 
Democrats) is more likely than a divided government ( in which the 
House flips to Democrats , while the Senate remains under 
Republican control). A scenario where Republicans retain a majority 
is least likely per prediction markets. 
While mid -term elections may introduce near -term volatility, we 
caution against overreaction. Historically, the average return for the 
S&P 500 following an opposition sweep is 17% over the next 12 
months, but the average return following a divided government is not 
significantly worse, at 15%. We prefer to base our investment views 
on fundamental earnings growth, where investor attention should 
focus on the upcoming Q3 earnings season. 
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is the outlook for  the memory chip and broader 
semiconductors industry? 
Our view: We remain positive on semiconductors and memory 
chips. Structural AI demand should prolong the memory 
upcycle while long-term agreements improve revenue visibility. 
Rationale: Recent industry commentary points to tighter memory 
supply-demand conditions through 2028, reinforcing our view that AI 
demand is likely to extend the memory upcycle. Furthermore, adoption 
of long-term agreements improves revenue and earnings visibility. 
Despite debates around “Pacing the Frontier”, we have seen a flurry 
of new model releases, along with the introduction of new consumer 
AI agents capable of comparing prices online and making 
purchases. These are early days, but the path is open for a business-
to-agent (B2A) economy where businesses increasingly interact with 
AI assistants acting on customers’ behalf. As these applications are 
increasingly inference intensive, wider adoption should support 
demand for memory, with its share of global semiconductor 
revenues projected to rise from 30.7% in 2025 to 66.7% in 2027. 
Against this backdrop, we maintain our Opportunistic idea on Global 
Semiconductors. Given memory  chips’ cyclical nature, investors 
should avoid overconcentration in memory names and maintain a 
diversified exposure across Big Tech and semiconductors. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  Does a US bond yield stress test raise the risk to your 
constructive view on US equities? 
Our view: We have a target for the S&P 500 index of 8400 over 
the next 12 months. A scenario of a sustained rise in the US 10-
year government bond yield to 5.6% could lead to negative 
returns from the current S&P500 level (7,666). 
Rationale: Our base case 12-month target for the S&P 500 is 8,400, 
which assumes 2027 S&P 500 EPS of USD 420 and a 20x forward 
P/E multiple. A rise in bond yields would have an impact on equity 
valuations, as a higher discount rate reduces the present value of 
future earnings. W e stress -test our target under different 10 -year 
yield and earnings scenarios.  
A rise in bond yields would arguably be associated with a stronger 
growth environment and hence, even higher earnings. However, 
assuming unchanged EPS at our base case of USD 420, a 
(sustained) rise in the 10 -year bond yield to 5.6% would lead to 
negative returns from the current level  if not offset by a further 
improvement in earnings growth . However, in o ur base case  we 
expect the 10-year yield is more likely to fall from here. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist

Document 449: The FOMC pause to reflect (2026-11-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/The FOMC pause to reflect (2026-11-11).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

#fomc #usd #us
The FOMC pause to reflect
By Elliot Clarke
Head of International Economics, Westpac
08:14 October 29 2026 Share
No change in October, though confidence in the economy's strength and two dissents point
to a hike in December.
The FOMC held at their October meeting, albeit with two dissents, Governor Cook and Cleveland Fed
President Hammack preferring to hike by 25bps at this meeting. The statement was short and, in the
press conference, Chair Warsh again declined to provide definitive guidance on the near-term policy

### Page 2

Related articles
outlook.
The Committee remains positive on economic activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding
at a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in September). Despite elevated uncertainty, “domestic
spending has been resilient”, underpinned by “strong” productivity growth and “robust” capital
investment. Chair Warsh subsequently also emphasised the breadth of economic growth outside of
tech-related investment. Labour demand and supply were represented as balanced and resilient in

both the statement and press conference.
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in the statement. However, inflation “remains
elevated” and the FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable return of annual
consumer inflation to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target.
Along with the two dissents, this guidance and the “accumulation of more information will help the
Committee determine the policy stance” suggests another 25bp hike is likely in December. Thereafter,

the FOMC will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related risks through 2027.
Towards the end of the press conference, Chair Warsh also announced a structural change in the
FOMC schedule. While there will still be 8 meetings in 2027, a meeting in the fall will focus on
monetary policy strategy versus the current stance of monetary policy. Chair Warsh then intends for 2
of the 8 meetings in 2028 to have this strategy focus. If realised, this will see the FOMC review and

vote on the current stance of monetary policy just 6 times per calendar year from 2028.

Document 496: 【國泰證期研究部】IP產業-IP重要度提升，各家廠商積極串聯價值鏈 -20261104

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】IP產業-IP重要度提升，各家廠商積極串聯價值鏈 -20261104.pdf

### Page 1

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
2026/11/04

1 
產業更新 – IP產業 
IP重要度提升，各家廠商積極串聯價值鏈  
What’s New –AI與先進製程提高 IP的策略價值 
大型 AI運算系統除了需要高效能運算核心，還需要 CPU負責控制與調度、HBM 提供高速資料
存取、SerDes 支援晶片及系統互連，以及安全模組進行身分驗證與金鑰保護，這意味著第三方
IP的商業機會並不只集中在單一晶片。但同時晶圓製程越先進，業者必須提前兩至三年投入研發，
卻因客戶專案延期而無法及時取得收入，短期獲利反而可能受到壓抑，研究部認為影響 IP 公司
競爭力的主要因素將是能否提早取得關鍵製程驗證、能否進入大型客戶的設計平台，以及技術投
入最終能否轉化為持續量產收入。 
Trends/Outlook –下游積極往上做併購取得關鍵 IP 
除了 IP供應商向下游擴張，晶片公司與晶圓代工廠也在向上游取得關鍵 IP。Qualcomm完成
收購 Alphawave Semi，強化高速傳輸、客製化晶片與資料中心產品能力。GlobalFoundries完
成收購 Synopsys的 ARC Processor IP 業務，並與 MIPS的 RISC-V、軟體及晶片開發能力結
合。皆說明 IP對下游業者的價值正在提高。反映晶圓代工與設計服務業者，也希望向客戶提供
更完整的晶片開發解決方案。因此未來產業競爭不只是IP公司彼此競爭，還包括晶片設計公司、
晶圓代工廠及 EDA業者在不同環節的跨界競爭。 
Investment Summary 
Synopsys、Cadence擁有龐大的全球客戶基礎，並能結合EDA工具、IP與驗證平台。ARM則
具有通用處理器架構及廣泛的軟體生態系。以台廠而言，M31(6643 TT) 主要產品涵蓋基礎元
件與高速介面 IP，並與多家晶圓代工廠合作開發不同製程的IP，面對AI ASIC與 Chiplet發展，
M31 有機會參與更多系統級晶片設計案。晶心科(6533 TT) 是台灣少數具有完整處理器 IP 產
品及相關工具生態系的上市公司，ARM直接跨足 CPU市場後，部分晶片設計業者可能更重視
供應商選擇與架構自主性，對 RISC-V形成間接利多。力旺(3529 TT) 其核心產品包括 OTP、
MTP、PUF等技術，可用於晶片內部資料儲存、修復、組態設定與安全用途。當ARM、Cadence
或其他大型供應商推出更完整的運算平台時，需要整合安全與記憶體功能，力旺已加入 ARM 
Total Design生態系，也參與Cadence的 Chiplet 合作平台，朝向更深入的系統級合作發展。
